# E1-T01 · S01 contract: OpenAPI of the identity endpoints, ErrorCode additions, snapshot

## Objective
The complete API contract of AgilityHub ID in the snapshot before the implementation tasks start, so the web repo can generate types in parallel.

## Spec references
`docs/specs/S01-agilityhub-id-i-acces.md` §6 (every endpoint with roles, bodies, responses and error codes), §3 (entities), §5 (states) · `docs/specs/00-transversal/CATALEG_ERRORS.md` (S01 row) · `docs/specs/00-transversal/CONVENCIONS_API.md`.

## Context to load
S01 §3, §5, §6 · CATALEG_ERRORS · CONVENCIONS_API · the existing `docs/openapi/openapi.json` and `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (E0-T12) · this file.

## Steps
1. Controllers with request/response DTOs and springdoc annotations for: `POST /oauth2/token` (document the custom grants and their form parameters as an `x-grants` extension), `GET /.well-known/openid-configuration`, `/.well-known/jwks.json`, `POST /oauth2/revoke`, `GET /oauth2/userinfo`, `GET /connect/logout`, `POST /auth/magic-link`, `POST /auth/handoff`, `GET /me`, `PATCH /me`, `PUT /me/password`, `PUT /me/profile`, `GET /me/sessions`, `DELETE /me/sessions/{id}`, `GET/PUT /me/onboarding`, `POST /members/{id}/impersonation-token`, `POST /platform/accounts`, `PUT /accounts/{id}/password`. Bodies exactly as S01 §6. Create the controllers now with real method signatures and `@Operation` docs; bodies not yet implemented throw `UnsupportedOperationException` (mapped to `501`) until E1-T02…T06 fill them — the point is a stable contract.
2. `ErrorCode`: every S01 code of the catalog (`INVALID_CREDENTIALS`, `LOGIN_LOCKED`, `NO_MEMBERSHIP`, `MEMBERSHIP_SUSPENDED`, `ACCOUNT_BLOCKED`, `MAGIC_LINK_INVALID`, `HANDOFF_INVALID`, `REFRESH_EXPIRED`, `PROFILE_NOT_AVAILABLE`, `PASSWORD_TOO_SHORT`, `PASSWORD_MISMATCH`, `PASSWORD_COMPROMISED`, `LOCALE_NOT_SUPPORTED`, `IMPERSONATION_DENIED`, `EMAIL_EXISTS`, …) — the catalog contract test must stay green.
3. Schemas `Me` (R-01-15 shape), `TokenResponse`, `Profile`, `Session`, `OnboardingState`, `HandoffResponse`, `ImpersonationTokenResponse`, `PlatformAccountRequest`.
4. `bin/openapi-snapshot` → commit `docs/openapi/openapi.json`; lines in `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md`.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `./mvnw -q verify` green (contract tests, catalog tests).
- `jq '.paths | keys' docs/openapi/openapi.json` → all routes above.
- `bin/openapi-snapshot && git status --short docs/openapi` → clean on a second run.

## Definition of Done
Contract complete and committed · error codes present · snapshot clean.

## Evidence to return
Outputs above; the list of new `ErrorCode` constants.

---

## Executor report
Completed E1-T01: 19 identity paths / 21 identity operations (including the existing JWKS alias), typed S01 request/response contracts, catalog-backed errors, executable authorization/tenant boundaries and standard 501 placeholders. Existing E0 club password/refresh and JWKS behavior is retained; club bootstrap now uses R-01-15. Business workflows remain assigned to E1-T02…T06.

### Branch / commits
Working tree only. No git write commands, branches or commits. The publish script will commit and push the session. Dependency E0-T12 was `verified` when this task started.

### Evidence (full command outputs)
Java 21.0.12.1; Docker 29.7.2. Maven's default wrapper cache is outside the writable sandbox, so successful Maven/snapshot commands used these environment settings (no repository tooling changes):

```sh
export MAVEN_USER_HOME=/tmp/agilityhub-maven-user-home
export MAVEN_OPTS=-Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/agilityhub-maven-repository
```

Both full `verify` runs exited 0. Final reports contain 158 unit tests and 116 integration/contract tests, 0 failures/errors/skips. This includes 22 `IdentityContractIT` cases and 6 `OpenApiSnapshotTest` cases. JaCoCo verification passed; identity API lines 94/107 (87.85%), branches 38/40 (95%); shared API lines 222/229 (96.94%). Full outputs below retain all lines, with hexadecimal token/hash identifiers truncated as required by AGENTS.md §6. Trailing log whitespace is removed for Markdown. Quiet Maven prints no BUILD SUCCESS banner; exit codes are recorded separately.

### Files changed
- `CHANGELOG.md`

- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/MeController.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/MeResponse.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/shared/api/ApiExceptionHandler.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/shared/api/TenantFilter.java`
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/configuration/OpenApiSnapshotTest.java`
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/MeIT.java`
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/shared/api/TenantFilterTest.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/AccountAdministrationController.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/AuthController.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/IdentityRequests.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/IdentityResponses.java`
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/OAuthController.java`
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/IdentityContractIT.java`
- `roadmap/STATUS.md`: regenerated by the roadmap tool (generated/ignored by repository policy).

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- R-01-03/04/06/11/13: typed token form, all five grants and `x-grants` fields; optional refresh/ID tokens; discovery, authorize/PKCE, scoped userinfo and logout signatures; magic-link and handoff bodies/statuses. New grants use the 501 MVC contract, existing SAS filters continue to handle E0 grants/JWKS. Contract coverage: `T_01_08_magicLinkContractIsPublicAndBindsTheSpecifiedSnakeCaseFields`, `T_01_13_oidcContractsAreGlobalAndUserInfoRequiresOpenIdScope`, `T_01_24_pendingGrantsReachThe501ContractWithoutChangingExistingGrants`.
- R-01-05/07/09/10/12/14: account/password/profile/session/impersonation/provisioning signatures, role/scope/impersonation guards and documented canonical errors. `T_01_09_passwordBodyRequiresNewAndRepeatAndProfileRequiresRemember`, `T_01_11_impersonationAndPasswordContractsRejectImpersonatedAccounts`, `T_01_14_accountWriteContractsRequireLearnAudienceAndScopeOrPlatformAdmin`. Workflow effects, resource ownership lookup, outbox/audit, emails and password checks are deliberately not implemented in this contract task.
- R-01-15/17: `Me` has account/platformRoles, current membership/clubId, activeProfile/profiles/defaultProfile/rememberProfile, optional instructorId/impersonation and features. Existing `MeIT` tests now assert the S01 fields and accept any valid account token; current membership and tenant mismatches remain checked. `T_01_17_protectedContractsRequireAuthenticationAndEnforceTenantBoundaries` covers every new authenticated endpoint with anonymous, authorized, mismatched-host and absent-club requests; global account administration remains global. `T_01_17_signedPlatformRoleAndScopeClaimsReachTheirAuthorizedContracts` checks actual signed bearer claims, not only mocked authorities.
- T-01-25: `T_01_25_identityContractMatchesS01BodiesRolesAndResponseSchemas` asserts paths/methods, security, tags, operation descriptions, all named schemas, exact specified request fields, Me membership fields, five grant definitions, Retry-After, array sessions, empty mutation/redirect bodies and the standard 501 envelope. Existing snapshot tests continue to check every schema reference and generation independence from host/language/port.
- S01 §14 / T-01-26: GET/PUT onboarding DTOs and authenticated 501 contracts are present; consent persistence and one-time behavior remain E1-T06.
- CATALEG_ERRORS contract and all three localized message catalogs pass unchanged. **New ErrorCode constants: none.** All 17 S01 constants already existed: `INVALID_CREDENTIALS` (401), `LOGIN_LOCKED` (429), `NO_MEMBERSHIP` (403), `MEMBERSHIP_SUSPENDED` (403), `ACCOUNT_BLOCKED` (403), `MAGIC_LINK_INVALID` (400), `HANDOFF_INVALID` (400), `REFRESH_EXPIRED` (400), `REFRESH_REUSED` (400), `PROFILE_NOT_AVAILABLE` (422), `PASSWORD_TOO_SHORT` (400), `PASSWORD_MISMATCH` (400), `PASSWORD_COMPROMISED` (400), `LOCALE_NOT_SUPPORTED` (400), `IMPERSONATION_DENIED` (403), `EMAIL_EXISTS` (409), `RATE_LIMITED` (429). The existing `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` (501) maps UnsupportedOperationException to the localized shared ApiError; no ad-hoc error body is added.

### Assumptions
- Paths follow CONVENCIONS_API §1 and S01 §6: OAuth/OIDC and `/auth/magic-link` at the identity root; other application paths at `/api/v1`, including `/api/v1/auth/handoff`. `/oauth2/authorize` is included because it appears in S01 §6 and the objective requires the complete identity contract, despite its omission from the step-1 enumeration. Existing `/oauth2/jwks` is retained as an alias.
- `Profile` represents MEMBER/INSTRUCTOR/ADMIN enum values, because S01 supplies role-based profiles without a separate profile object shape. Sessions are a bounded account-device array, not a desktop paginated resource; ids are public opaque revocation handles, never stored token hashes. Only the public session metadata from §3 is exposed.
- S01 §6 gives no success body for PATCH `/me` or account provisioning: assumed `Me` and public `AccountSummary` including `id`, respectively. Revocation, password changes and session deletion have empty success bodies with the specified 200 status. Authorization-code form includes `code`/`redirect_uri`; `code_verifier` and `client_secret` have client-dependent requirements described in the operation. The token schema describes the complete E1 response, including scope; existing E0 public-client restrictions remain until the implementation task.
- S01 §6 has no onboarding endpoint/body table. The requested GET/PUT contract follows §14: state `{onboardingPending, name?, locale?, phone?, requestedFields[], privacyPolicyVersion, privacyPolicyUrl}`; input `{name?, locale?, phone?, privacyAccepted, privacyPolicyVersion, imageConsent?}`. Missing data may be deferred; accepted current privacy policy is mandatory in the future service. No parameter, consent event or notification was invented or implemented.
- E0 Membership does not persist instructorId/rememberProfile. The existing club bootstrap reads instructorId/activeProfile from signed claims, derives profiles from membership roles, and reports rememberProfile=false until E1-T02 adds preference persistence. Optional null fields are omitted. Global and impersonated bootstrap return 501 until their services exist. Existing club membership/account validation remains active; pending handlers do not mutate data.
- The closed error catalog wins over S01 prose: REFRESH_EXPIRED is 400, not the 401 example in R-01-06. `invalid_grant`/`invalid_request` remain protocol descriptions, not new ErrorCode constants; the API uses the catalog codes and shared envelope.
- Git cleanliness after generation means no additional snapshot changes on regeneration, proved by byte comparison. The two API files necessarily remain modified until the external publish script commits them; no git write was attempted.

### Questions / catalog proposals
No catalog additions or business-rule decisions. Nonblocking wire-format assumptions for onboarding, Profile, Session and unspecified success bodies are addressed to @organizer in `roadmap/MESSAGES.md` for confirmation before E1-T02/E1-T06. All implementation remains within E1-T01.

### Round 2

Completed the three numbered corrections from Organizer verification. This round supersedes the initial route and onboarding assumptions above; no other roadmap task was started. Dependency E0-T12 remains verified. The working tree was clean at session start; no git write commands were run.

#### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)

1. S01 §6/§14, T-01-26: `OnboardingState {pending, postponeRemaining, requiredConsent, fields[]}`; consent and field values explicitly support JSON null in serialization and the OpenAPI 3.1 type unions. Required consent has PLATFORM/CLUB policy, version and URL. Input is `{consentAccepted, consentVersion, fields?: {name?, locale?, phone?}, imageConsent?}`. Missing/false acceptance and missing/blank version return catalog `VALIDATION_ERROR`; LOCALE_NOT_SUPPORTED (400) and CONSENT_VERSION_OUTDATED (422) are documented for the future service. Added authenticated POST `/api/v1/me/onboarding/postpone`, returning standard 501 until E1-T06. `T_01_26_onboardingUsesNestedFieldsAndRequiresAcceptedVersionedConsent` and `T_01_26_onboardingSerializationRetainsExplicitNullConsentAndFieldValues` cover binding, each club role, and explicit nullable output. Extended `T_01_17_protectedContractsRequireAuthenticationAndEnforceTenantBoundaries` to cover postpone with anonymous, authorized, global-account and mismatched-host requests. The approved `signup.onboardingFields` and `legal.maxPostpones` entries/defaults already exist in catalog.yaml from E1-T03; the unchanged `T_02_03_catalogMatchesDocumentKeysTypesAndActualDefaults` passes for all 148 keys.
2. R-01-04 / T-01-08, T-01-15: moved magic-link mapping, public security matcher and optional-host tenant handling to `/api/v1/auth/magic-link`. Added the route to the existing authentication IP quota. Updated `T_01_08_magicLinkContractIsPublicAndBindsTheSpecifiedSnakeCaseFields`; new `T_01_15_magicLinkUsesTheAuthenticationIpQuotaBeforeTenantResolution` verifies 429/Retry-After, shared token quota, security event, independent IP and quota refill.
3. R-01-15 / T-01-25: `MeAccount` adds required hasPassword/onboardingPending and optional emailVerifiedAt; MembershipSummary adds optional MALE/FEMALE/OTHER gender. The separate provisioning AccountSummary keeps its confirmed shape. Existing Me role/tenant tests check the new required flags. `T_01_25_passwordlessAccountHasRequiredFalseFlagsAndNoMemberGender` covers the missing-hash case and absent member; `T_01_25_bootstrapSerializesOptionalVerifiedTimeAndMemberGenderWithoutCredentials` covers all three genders and an ISO-8601 verified timestamp. Responses exclude passwordHash/security/tokens/externalIds. Expanded `T_01_25_identityContractMatchesS01BodiesRolesAndResponseSchemas` checks exact fields, required/optional flags, null types, response/error schemas, new/removed routes and unchanged provisioning fields.

#### Assumptions

- S01 v0.3 §6 and the explicit correction take precedence over the stale root magic-link example in CONVENCIONS_API §1. That source document was not edited.
- This remains a contract task. Valid onboarding requests (including postpone) return standard 501 and do not persist consent, profile values or counters; E1-T06 implements those workflows. `@AssertTrue` only enforces the specified request acceptance constraint.
- Current `Account` has no emailVerifiedAt field and there is no Member lookup in the E0 bootstrap. These optional DTO/schema fields are serialization-tested but omitted by the current controller until their backing services/models exist. hasPassword derives from non-null passwordHash. onboardingPending is explicitly false until E1-T06, as requested, even for a persisted pending flag. No persistence model was expanded in this contract correction.
- No dedicated magic-link rate parameter exists in the loaded catalog. The new route shares the existing configurable TOKEN IP bucket with `/oauth2/token`; no new quota/default/configuration key was invented. Security-event route labels retain their existing normalization.
- Both approved onboarding parameters were registered by E1-T03 before this round. Their types/defaults were checked against the current catalog and the full catalog contract passed; no duplicate YAML entries or value changes were needed.
- A clean second snapshot run means byte-for-byte equality with the first generation. Git status necessarily lists the intentional uncommitted API changes until the external publish script commits them.

#### Questions / catalog proposals

None. All three organizer corrections are applied. New ErrorCode constants: none; the documented consent error already exists in the closed catalog. No new parameter, event, notification or error code was added. No other task file, ROADMAP.md or Organizer verification section was edited.

Solid contract round: the 22 paths cover every S01 §6 row (plus `/oauth2/authorize`, correctly included), `Me` matches R-01-15, the five grants are documented as `x-grants`, the 501 envelope reuses `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`, no catalog code invented, `IdentityContractIT` (22 cases) + snapshot regeneration proven byte-identical, both `verify` runs green (158 unit + 116 IT). The wire assumptions you asked about are confirmed except where listed below — everything else stays as shipped: `Profile` = role enum (`MEMBER|INSTRUCTOR|ADMIN`), sessions = bounded array of public device metadata with opaque `id`, `PATCH /me` → `Me`, `POST /platform/accounts` → `AccountSummary`, empty bodies for the other successful mutations, `REFRESH_EXPIRED` = 400 (catalog wins). Three corrections so the web can generate types once from this snapshot (spec S01 updated to v0.3 in `docs/` with exactly these texts):

1. **Onboarding contract per S01 §14 / E1-T06, not the ad-hoc shape.** `GET /me/onboarding` → `200 OnboardingState {pending: boolean, postponeRemaining: integer, requiredConsent: {policy: "PLATFORM"|"CLUB", version: string, url: string} | null, fields: OnboardingField[]}` with `OnboardingField {key: string, value: string|null, required: boolean}` (keys from the new catalog parameter `signup.onboardingFields`, default `name`/`locale` required + `phone` optional). `PUT /me/onboarding` body `OnboardingRequest {consentAccepted: boolean, consentVersion: string, fields?: {name?: string, locale?: string, phone?: string}, imageConsent?: boolean}` → `200 OnboardingState`; errors `400 VALIDATION_ERROR` (consent not accepted), `422 CONSENT_VERSION_OUTDATED`, `400 LOCALE_NOT_SUPPORTED`. Add `POST /me/onboarding/postpone` → `200 OnboardingState` (501 for now, like the rest). Drop `onboardingPending/privacyPolicy*/requestedFields` from these schemas. Parameters `signup.onboardingFields` (json) and `legal.maxPostpones` (int, 3) are now in `docs/specs/00-transversal/CATALEG_PARAMETRES.md`; register both in `parameters/catalog.yaml` in this round (values only; behaviour is E1-T06).
